View lessGemini offers two sets of offerings. These are Google AI plans for general consumers and Gemini for Google Cloud for business and developer use. They have different subscription models and usage integrations. 
Gemini for Personal Use (Google AI Plans)
Premium Gemini features for personal use are part of Google AI plans (Plus, Pro, and Ultra). These are integrated with a Google Account and managed through Google One. 
Subscription Model: A monthly fee is charged (e.g., Google AI Pro includes 2 TB of storage).
Included Benefits: Access to more capable models (like Gemini 1.5 Pro/3 Pro), features within Gmail, Docs, and other Google apps, and expanded Google Drive storage.
Cloud Usage: These personal plans do not require a Google Cloud project or managing API usage/billing in the Google Cloud console directly. However, they do provide higher daily usage limits for specific tools like the Gemini Code Assist IDE extension for individuals. 
Gemini for Business & Developers (Gemini for Google Cloud) 
For organizations and professional developers, Gemini is available as "Gemini for Google Cloud." This is deeply integrated with the Google Cloud platform. 
Subscription Model: This is a separate suite of products (e.g., Gemini Code Assist Standard/Enterprise, Gemini in BigQuery, Gemini in Security). It is billed via a Google Cloud billing account on a per-user/per-hour basis or via API token consumption.
Cloud Usage: Usage is tied directly to a Google Cloud project, which manages API access, quotas, and billing. Costs are based on actual consumption (e.g., number of input/output tokens for API usage).
Data Handling: Prompts and responses are not used to train Google's models for paid enterprise plans. 
Summary of Differences
Feature 	Personal Google AI Plans (via Google One)	Gemini for Google Cloud (via GCP Console)
Target User	Individual consumers	Businesses, teams, and developers
Billing	Monthly subscription fee (e.g., $X/month)	Pay-as-you-go or per-user license
Integration	Gmail, Docs, Drive, Gemini App, etc.	Google Cloud Console, IDEs (VS Code, IntelliJ), BigQuery, Security
Data Usage	Can opt out of data use for training on the free tier; paid plans generally don't use data for training	Data is handled according to enterprise terms and not used for model training
The plan and billing method depend on the use case. Personal productivity is through Google AI plans/Google One. Building applications or using AI within cloud services is managed through Google Cloud subscriptions and billing. https://cloud.google.com/products/gemini/pricing18:06

Good — the screenshot confirms **billing account is PAID** and **£10 credit is active**.  
However, this **alone is not sufficient** to lift the *Gemini API free-tier quota*. There are **two remaining gates** that commonly cause the exact 429 you’re still seeing.

---

## Gate 1 (most common): **Billing not attached to the *project***  
What you showed is the **billing account**, not the **project linkage**.

### Verify (30 seconds):
1. In **Google Cloud Console**
2. Top bar → **Select the VoxCortex project** (exact one used by your API key)
3. Go to **Billing**
4. Confirm it says:  
   **“Billing account: My Billing Account (ACTIVE)”**

If it says **“No billing account”** → attach it.

> 🔎 Why this matters: API quotas are enforced **per project**, not per billing account.

---

## Gate 2 (very common): **Gemini / Generative Language API not enabled**
Even with billing attached, the API can remain in free-tier mode if the service isn’t enabled.

### Enable explicitly:
1. **APIs & Services → Library**
2. Search **“Generative Language API”** (Gemini)
3. Click **Enable**
4. Wait ~1–3 minutes

---

## Propagation delay (real, but short)
After both gates are correct:
- Quota propagation takes **2–5 minutes**
- During this window, you may still see:
  ```
  generate_content_free_tier_requests
  ```
- This should **disappear** once propagation completes.

---

```

**Success criteria:**
- ❌ You should NOT see `free_tier_requests`
- ✅ Either:

  - A *different* quota metric appears (RPM/TPM), which we can tune

If you still see `free_tier_requests` after **both gates + 5 minutes**, then the API key is likely pointing to a **different project** than the one you billed.

# 🧩 CODE — PolicyGate (drop-in)

```python
# services/policy/policy_gate.py

import json
from typing import Any, Dict, List


class PolicyViolation(Exception):
    pass


class PolicyGate:
    """
    Phase-1 Policy Gate.
    Enforces that LLM output is:
    - JSON only
    - Schema compliant
    - Non-actionable
    """

    REQUIRED_FIELDS = {
        "explanation": str,
        "confidence_language": dict,
        "evidence_ids": list,
        "what_would_change_my_mind": list,
    }

    FORBIDDEN_KEYWORDS = [
        "INSERT",
        "UPDATE",
        "DELETE",
        "DROP",
        "EXEC",
        "CALL",
        "TOOL",
        "HTTP",
        "REQUEST",
        "POST",
        "WRITE",
    ]

    @classmethod
    def validate(cls, raw_output: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Validate and sanitize LLM output.
        Returns validated JSON dict or raises PolicyViolation.
        """

        # 1. Must be valid JSON
        try:
            payload = json.loads(raw_output)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            raise PolicyViolation(f"Output is not valid JSON: {e}")

        # 2. Must be a JSON object
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            raise PolicyViolation("Output JSON must be an object")

        # 3. Required fields check
        for field, expected_type in cls.REQUIRED_FIELDS.items():
            if field not in payload:
                raise PolicyViolation(f"Missing required field: {field}")
            if not isinstance(payload[field], expected_type):
                raise PolicyViolation(
                    f"Field '{field}' must be of type {expected_type.__name__}"
                )

        # 4. evidence_ids must be list[str]
        for eid in payload["evidence_ids"]:
            if not isinstance(eid, str):
                raise PolicyViolation("evidence_ids must be a list of strings")

        # 5. Scan for forbidden language
        raw_upper = raw_output.upper()
        for word in cls.FORBIDDEN_KEYWORDS:
            if word in raw_upper:
                raise PolicyViolation(f"Forbidden keyword detected: {word}")

        return payload
```

---

- Gemini **cannot escape JSON**
- Gemini **cannot bypass evidence**
- Any violation = **hard stop**

No opinions. No heuristics. Deterministic.

# 1) Wire `PolicyGate` into Gemini reasoner

### A) Ensure file exists
**`services/policy/policy_gate.py`** (use this exact version)

```python
import json
from typing import Any, Dict


class PolicyViolation(Exception):
    pass


class PolicyGate:
    """
    Phase-1 Policy Gate.
    Enforces that LLM output is:
      - JSON object
      - Required schema
      - Non-actionable language (no DB/tool verbs)
    """

    REQUIRED_FIELDS = {
        "explanation": str,
        "confidence_language": dict,
        "evidence_ids": list,
        "what_would_change_my_mind": list,
    }

    FORBIDDEN_KEYWORDS = [
        "INSERT", "UPDATE", "DELETE", "DROP", "ALTER", "CREATE",
        "EXEC", "CALL", "TOOL", "FUNCTION", "HTTP", "REQUEST", "POST",
        "WRITE", "RUN", "SHELL", "PSQL",
    ]

    @classmethod
    def validate(cls, raw_output: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            payload = json.loads(raw_output)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            raise PolicyViolation(f"Output is not valid JSON: {e}") from e

        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            raise PolicyViolation("Output JSON must be an object")

        for field, expected_type in cls.REQUIRED_FIELDS.items():
            if field not in payload:
                raise PolicyViolation(f"Missing required field: {field}")
            if not isinstance(payload[field], expected_type):
                raise PolicyViolation(
                    f"Field '{field}' must be of type {expected_type.__name__}"
                )

        # evidence_ids must be list[str]
        for eid in payload["evidence_ids"]:
            if not isinstance(eid, str):
                raise PolicyViolation("evidence_ids must be a list of strings")

        raw_upper = raw_output.upper()
        for word in cls.FORBIDDEN_KEYWORDS:
            if word in raw_upper:
                raise PolicyViolation(f"Forbidden keyword detected: {word}")

        return payload
```

# Extract text from SDK response (keeps this tolerant to minor SDK differences)
raw_text = getattr(response, "text", None)
if not raw_text:
    # some SDK versions store candidates; keep a safe fallback
    raw_text = str(response)

try:

    return validated
except PolicyViolation as e:
    logger.warning("PolicyGate rejected LLM output: %s", e)

    # Deterministic safe fallback (Phase-1: AI reasoning allowed, but never actions)
    return {
        "explanation": "POLICYGATE_REJECTED: LLM output failed validation. Falling back to safe non-actionable explanation.",
        "confidence_language": {
            "level": "unknown",
            "calibration": "blocked_by_policy_gate"
        },
        "evidence_ids": [],

        ],

**`tests/test_policy_gate.py`**

```python
import json
import pytest

from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation


def valid_payload():
    return {
        "explanation": "Because evidence A supports claim X under rule R.",
        "confidence_language": {"level": "medium", "calibration": "bounded"},
        "evidence_ids": ["e1", "e2"],
        "what_would_change_my_mind": ["New evidence contradicting e1/e2."],
    }


def test_accepts_valid_json_payload():
    raw = json.dumps(valid_payload())
    out = PolicyGate.validate(raw)
    assert out["explanation"].startswith("Because")
    assert out["evidence_ids"] == ["e1", "e2"]


def test_rejects_non_json():
    with pytest.raises(PolicyViolation):
        PolicyGate.validate("not json at all")


def test_rejects_non_object_json():
    with pytest.raises(PolicyViolation):
        PolicyGate.validate(json.dumps(["not", "an", "object"]))


def test_rejects_missing_fields():
    p = valid_payload()
    del p["evidence_ids"]
    with pytest.raises(PolicyViolation) as e:
        PolicyGate.validate(json.dumps(p))
    assert "Missing required field" in str(e.value)


def test_rejects_wrong_type():
    p = valid_payload()
    p["evidence_ids"] = "e1"  # wrong
    with pytest.raises(PolicyViolation):
        PolicyGate.validate(json.dumps(p))


def test_rejects_non_string_evidence_ids():
    p = valid_payload()
    p["evidence_ids"] = ["e1", 2]
    with pytest.raises(PolicyViolation):
        PolicyGate.validate(json.dumps(p))


def test_rejects_forbidden_keywords():
    p = valid_payload()
    p["explanation"] = "We should UPDATE the database now."  # forbidden token
    with pytest.raises(PolicyViolation) as e:
        PolicyGate.validate(json.dumps(p))
    assert "Forbidden keyword" in str(e.value)
```

Run tests from repo root:
```bash
pytest -q

```python
import json
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation


def build_hypothesis_prompt(belief: Dict[str, Any], evidence: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    """
    Prompt is bounded: hypotheses only, no actions, must cite evidence_ids.
    """
    evidence_ids = [e.get("evidence_id") for e in evidence if e.get("evidence_id")]
    return f"""
You are a reasoning module. You MUST NOT propose actions, tool calls, or DB writes.

Return STRICT JSON with fields:
{{
  "hypotheses": [
     {{
       "id": "H1",
       "statement": "...",
       "supporting_evidence_ids": ["..."],
       "counter_evidence_ids": ["..."],
       "tests_or_observations_needed": ["..."]
     }}
  ]
}}

Context:
belief={json.dumps(belief, ensure_ascii=False)}
known_evidence_ids={json.dumps(evidence_ids, ensure_ascii=False)}

Now generate 2-4 competing hypotheses.
""".strip()


def parse_and_gate(raw_text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Uses PolicyGate for baseline safety (JSON-only, non-actionable language).
    You can extend with a stricter schema check later (Phase-1).
    """
    gated = PolicyGate.validate(raw_text)

    # Minimal shape check for hypotheses generator:
    if "hypotheses" not in gated or not isinstance(gated["hypotheses"], list):
        raise PolicyViolation("Hypothesis output must contain a list field 'hypotheses'")

    return gated
```

**Note:** This file does **not** call Gemini yet. It defines:
- prompt builder
- gated parser
So you can plug it into your existing Gemini client in the next commit without mixing concerns.

```python
import os
import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict

from google import genai

from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# -------------------------------------------------------------------

MODEL_PRIMARY = os.getenv(
    "GEMINI_REASONER_MODEL",
    "models/gemini-2.5-flash",
)

_client = genai.Client(
    [REDACTED:assigned-value:sha256-852aab0d2be3]("GEMINI_API_KEY")
)


# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# Phase-1 compliant explain() — REASONING ONLY, NO ACTIONS
# -------------------------------------------------------------------

def explain(
    *,
    trace_id: str,
    belief: Dict[str, Any],
    evidence: Dict[str, Any],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Phase-1 reasoning hook.
    - Gemini may reason
    - Gemini may NOT act
    - Output MUST pass PolicyGate
    """

    prompt = f"""
You are a reasoning component.

STRICT RULES:
- NO actions
- NO tools
- NO database operations
- OUTPUT JSON ONLY

Required JSON schema:
{{
  "explanation": "...",
  "confidence_language": {{ "...": "..." }},
  "evidence_ids": ["..."],
  "what_would_change_my_mind": ["..."]
}}

Context:
belief = {json.dumps(belief, ensure_ascii=False)}
evidence = {json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False)}
trace_id = "{trace_id}"

Return ONLY JSON.
""".strip()

    response = _client.models.generate_content(
        model=MODEL_PRIMARY,
        contents=prompt,
    )

    # SDK compatibility: prefer .text, fallback to str()
    raw_text = getattr(response, "text", None)
    if not raw_text:
        raw_text = str(response)

    try:
        validated = PolicyGate.validate(raw_text)
        logger.info("PolicyGate accepted Gemini output (trace=%s)", trace_id)
        return validated

    except PolicyViolation as e:
        logger.warning(
            "PolicyGate REJECTED Gemini output (trace=%s): %s",
            trace_id,
            e,
        )

        # Deterministic safe fallback (never actionable)
        return {
            "explanation": (
                "POLICYGATE_REJECTED: Gemini output violated Phase-1 policy. "
                "Returned safe non-actionable explanation."
            ),
            "confidence_language": {
                "level": "unknown",
                "calibration": "blocked_by_policy_gate",
            },
            "evidence_ids": [],
            "what_would_change_my_mind": [
                "Provide a policy-compliant JSON response citing evidence_ids "
                "and avoiding any action or tool language."
            ],
        }
```

import os
import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict

from google import genai

from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# -------------------------------------------------------------------

MODEL_PRIMARY = os.getenv(
    "GEMINI_REASONER_MODEL",
    "models/gemini-2.5-flash",
)

_client = genai.Client(
    [REDACTED:assigned-value:sha256-852aab0d2be3]("GEMINI_API_KEY")
)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# BACKWARD-COMPATIBLE explain()
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# Accepts BOTH:
#   explain(trace_id, belief, evidence)
#   explain(trace_id=..., belief=..., evidence=...)
#
# This preserves Phase-0 callers unchanged.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------

def explain(*args, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    # ---- Normalize inputs (positional OR keyword) ----
    if args:
        try:
            trace_id, belief, evidence = args
        except ValueError:
            raise TypeError(
                "explain() expects (trace_id, belief, evidence) "
                "or keyword arguments"
            )
    else:
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id")
        belief = kwargs.get("belief")
        evidence = kwargs.get("evidence")

    if trace_id is None or belief is None or evidence is None:
        raise TypeError(
            "explain() missing required arguments: trace_id, belief, evidence"
        )

    # ---- Phase-1 reasoning prompt (NO ACTIONS) ----
    prompt = f"""
You are a reasoning component.

STRICT RULES:
- NO actions
- NO tools
- NO database operations
- OUTPUT JSON ONLY

Required JSON schema:
{{
  "explanation": "...",
  "confidence_language": {{ "...": "..." }},
  "evidence_ids": ["..."],
  "what_would_change_my_mind": ["..."]
}}

Context:
belief = {json.dumps(belief, ensure_ascii=False)}
evidence = {json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False)}
trace_id = "{trace_id}"

Return ONLY JSON.
""".strip()

    response = _client.models.generate_content(
        model=MODEL_PRIMARY,
        contents=prompt,
    )

    raw_text = getattr(response, "text", None)
    if not raw_text:
        raw_text = str(response)

    try:
        validated = PolicyGate.validate(raw_text)
        logger.info("PolicyGate accepted Gemini output (trace=%s)", trace_id)
        return validated

    except PolicyViolation as e:
        logger.warning(
            "PolicyGate REJECTED Gemini output (trace=%s): %s",
            trace_id,
            e,
        )

        # Deterministic, non-actionable fallback
        return {
            "explanation": (
                "POLICYGATE_REJECTED: Gemini output violated Phase-1 policy. "
                "Returned safe non-actionable explanation."
            ),
            "confidence_language": {
                "level": "unknown",
                "calibration": "blocked_by_policy_gate",
            },
            "evidence_ids": [],
            "what_would_change_my_mind": [
                "Provide a policy-compliant JSON response citing evidence_ids "
                "and avoiding any action or tool language."
            ],
        }

```python
import os
import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict

from google import genai
from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# -------------------------------------------------------------------

MODEL_PRIMARY = os.getenv(
    "GEMINI_REASONER_MODEL",
    "models/gemini-2.5-flash",
)

_client = genai.Client(
    [REDACTED:assigned-value:sha256-852aab0d2be3]("GEMINI_API_KEY")
)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# BACKWARD-COMPATIBLE explain()
#
# Supports ALL of:
#   explain(belief, evidence)                 # Phase-0
#   explain(trace_id, belief, evidence)       # Phase-1+
#   explain(trace_id=..., belief=..., evidence=...)
# -------------------------------------------------------------------

def explain(*args, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:

    trace_id = None
    belief = None
    evidence = None

    # ---------- Positional handling ----------
    if len(args) == 2:
        # Phase-0 style: explain(belief, evidence)
        belief, evidence = args
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "phase0")

    elif len(args) == 3:
        # Phase-1 style: explain(trace_id, belief, evidence)
        trace_id, belief, evidence = args

    elif len(args) == 0:
        # Keyword-only
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "kw")
        belief = kwargs.get("belief")
        evidence = kwargs.get("evidence")

    else:
        raise TypeError(
            "explain() supports (belief, evidence) OR "
            "(trace_id, belief, evidence) OR keyword arguments"
        )

    if belief is None or evidence is None:
        raise TypeError(
            "explain() missing required belief and evidence arguments"
        )

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase-1-safe reasoning prompt (NO ACTIONS)
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------

    prompt = f"""
You are a reasoning component.

STRICT RULES:
- NO actions
- NO tools
- NO database operations
- OUTPUT JSON ONLY

Required JSON schema:
{{
  "explanation": "...",
  "confidence_language": {{ "...": "..." }},
  "evidence_ids": ["..."],
  "what_would_change_my_mind": ["..."]
}}

Context:
belief = {json.dumps(belief, ensure_ascii=False)}
evidence = {json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False)}
trace_id = "{trace_id}"

Return ONLY JSON.
""".strip()

import os
import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict

from google import genai
from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# -------------------------------------------------------------------

MODEL_PRIMARY = os.getenv(
    "GEMINI_REASONER_MODEL",
    "models/gemini-2.5-flash",
)

_client = genai.Client(
    [REDACTED:assigned-value:sha256-852aab0d2be3]("GEMINI_API_KEY")
)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# BACKWARD-COMPATIBLE explain()
#
# Supports:
#   explain(belief, evidence)                 # Phase-0
#   explain(trace_id, belief, evidence)       # Phase-1+
#   explain(trace_id=..., belief=..., evidence=...)
# -------------------------------------------------------------------

def explain(*args, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    trace_id = None
    belief = None
    evidence = None

    # ---- Normalize arguments ----
    if len(args) == 2:
        belief, evidence = args
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "phase0")

    elif len(args) == 3:
        trace_id, belief, evidence = args

    elif len(args) == 0:
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "kw")
        belief = kwargs.get("belief")
        evidence = kwargs.get("evidence")

    else:
        raise TypeError(
            "explain() supports (belief, evidence), "
            "(trace_id, belief, evidence), or keyword arguments"
        )

    if belief is None or evidence is None:
        raise TypeError(
            "explain() missing required belief and evidence arguments"
        )

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase-1-safe reasoning prompt (NO ACTIONS)
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------

    prompt = f"""
You are a reasoning component.

STRICT RULES:
- NO actions
- NO tools
- NO database operations
- OUTPUT JSON ONLY

Required JSON schema:
{{
  "explanation": "...",
  "confidence_language": {{ "...": "..." }},
  "evidence_ids": ["..."],
  "what_would_change_my_mind": ["..."]
}}

Context:
belief = {json.dumps(belief, ensure_ascii=False)}
evidence = {json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False)}
trace_id = "{trace_id}"

Return ONLY JSON.
""".strip()

    response = _client.models.generate_content(
        model=MODEL_PRIMARY,
        contents=prompt,
    )

    raw_text = getattr(response, "text", None)
    if not raw_text:
        raw_text = str(response)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------
    # PolicyGate enforcement
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------

    try:
        validated = PolicyGate.validate(raw_text)
        logger.info("PolicyGate accepted Gemini output (trace=%s)", trace_id)
        return validated

    except PolicyViolation as e:
        logger.warning(
            "PolicyGate REJECTED Gemini output (trace=%s): %s",
            trace_id,
            e,
        )

        return {
            "explanation": (
                "POLICYGATE_REJECTED: Gemini output violated Phase-1 policy. "
                "Returned safe non-actionable explanation."
            ),
            "confidence_language": {
                "level": "unknown",
                "calibration": "blocked_by_policy_gate",
            },
            "evidence_ids": [],
            "what_would_change_my_mind": [
                "Provide a policy-compliant JSON response citing evidence_ids "
                "and avoiding any action or tool language."
            ],
        }
```

- You used **PowerShell `@' … '@ > file.py`**
- Inside that heredoc you had:
  - triple-quoted Python strings
  - `{}` braces
  - quotes
- PowerShell **did not write this as clean UTF-8 text**
- It injected **NULL bytes**
- Python refuses to import any `.py` file with NULL bytes

That’s why:
- `type` *shows* the file
- `pytest` passes (doesn’t import this module)
- `python -m workers.phase0_worker` **fails at import time**

This is a **file encoding corruption**, not code.

    elif len(args) == 3:

    elif len(args) == 0:
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "kw")
        belief = kwargs.get("belief")
        evidence = kwargs.get("evidence")
    else:
        raise TypeError(
            "explain() supports (belief, evidence), "
            "(trace_id, belief, evidence), or keyword arguments"
        )

    if belief is None or evidence is None:
        raise TypeError("explain() missing required belief and evidence")

    prompt = f"""
You are a reasoning component.

STRICT RULES:
- NO actions
- NO tools
- NO database operations
- OUTPUT JSON ONLY

Required JSON schema:
{{
  "explanation": "...",
  "confidence_language": {{ "...": "..." }},
  "evidence_ids": ["..."],
  "what_would_change_my_mind": ["..."]
}}

Context:
belief = {json.dumps(belief, ensure_ascii=False)}
evidence = {json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False)}
trace_id = "{trace_id}"

Return ONLY JSON.
""".strip()

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from services.shared.config import Settings

_engine = None

def get_engine():
    global _engine
    if _engine is not None:
        return _engine

    settings = Settings()

    if not settings.database_url:
        raise RuntimeError("database_url is empty in config.py")

    _engine = create_engine(settings.database_url)
    return _engine
```

    )

    return _ENGINE

"""
DB Health Check — VoxCortex
Purpose:
- Prove DB connectivity
- Prove correct config precedence
- Prove SQL execution
NO pytest. NO mocks. NO abstractions.
"""

import os
from sqlalchemy import text
from services.shared.db import get_engine


def main():
    print("=== VoxCortex DB Health Check ===")

    # 1. Print config visibility (NO secrets)
    print("\n[Config visibility]")
    print("DATABASE_URL set:", bool(os.getenv("DATABASE_URL")))
    print("POSTGRES_PASSWORD set:", bool(os.getenv("POSTGRES_PASSWORD")))

    # 2. Create engine
    print("\n[Engine creation]")
    engine = get_engine()
    print("Engine OK:", engine)

    # 3. Open connection
    print("\n[Connection test]")
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        result = conn.execute(text("SELECT 1")).scalar()
        print("SELECT 1 result:", result)

    # 4. Optional: check core tables exist
    print("\n[Schema sanity]")
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        tables = conn.execute(
            text("""
                SELECT tablename
                FROM pg_tables
                WHERE schemaname = 'public'
                ORDER BY tablename;
            """)
        ).fetchall()

    print("Tables found:")
    for t in tables:
        print(" -", t[0])

    print("\n✅ DB HEALTH CHECK PASSED")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## ✅ **REPLACE ENTIRE FILE**

```python
import os
import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict

from google import genai
from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# -------------------------------------------------------------------

MODEL_PRIMARY = os.getenv(
    "GEMINI_REASONER_MODEL",
    "models/gemini-2.5-flash",
)

_client = genai.Client(
    [REDACTED:assigned-value:sha256-852aab0d2be3]("GEMINI_API_KEY")
)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# explain()
#
# Phase-1 ENABLED
# - AI reasoning allowed
# - NO actions
# - NO tools
# - PolicyGate enforced
#
# Backward compatible:
#   explain(belief, evidence)                 # Phase-0 caller
#   explain(trace_id, belief, evidence)       # Phase-1+
#   explain(trace_id=..., belief=..., evidence=...)
# -------------------------------------------------------------------

def explain(*args, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    trace_id = None
    belief = None
    evidence = None

    # -------- Argument normalization --------
    if len(args) == 2:
        belief, evidence = args
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "phase0")
    elif len(args) == 3:
        trace_id, belief, evidence = args
    elif len(args) == 0:
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "kw")
        belief = kwargs.get("belief")
        evidence = kwargs.get("evidence")
    else:
        raise TypeError(
            "explain() supports (belief, evidence), "
            "(trace_id, belief, evidence), or keyword arguments"
        )

    if belief is None or evidence is None:
        raise TypeError("explain() missing required belief and evidence")

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase-1 reasoning prompt (STRICT)
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------

    prompt = f"""
You are a reasoning component inside an enterprise system.

ABSOLUTE RULES:
- NO actions
- NO tools
- NO database operations
- OUTPUT VALID JSON ONLY
- DO NOT include markdown
- DO NOT include commentary

Required JSON schema:
{{
  "explanation": "...",
  "confidence_language": {{ "...": "..." }},
  "evidence_ids": ["..."],
  "what_would_change_my_mind": ["..."]
}}

Context:
belief = {json.dumps(belief, ensure_ascii=False)}
evidence = {json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False)}
trace_id = "{trace_id}"

Return ONLY the JSON object.
""".strip()

    response = _client.models.generate_content(
        model=MODEL_PRIMARY,
        contents=prompt,
    )

    raw_text = getattr(response, "text", None) or str(response)
    raw_text = raw_text.strip()

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------
    # HARD JSON EXTRACTION (NO TRUST)
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------

    start = raw_text.find("{")
    end = raw_text.rfind("}")

    if start == -1 or end == -1 or end <= start:
        raise PolicyViolation(
            f"No valid JSON object found in Gemini output (trace={trace_id})"
        )

    json_text = raw_text[start:end + 1]

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------
    # PolicyGate enforcement
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------

    try:
        validated = PolicyGate.validate(json_text)
        logger.info(
            "PolicyGate accepted Gemini output (trace=%s)", trace_id
        )
        return validated

    except PolicyViolation as e:
        logger.warning(
            "PolicyGate REJECTED Gemini output (trace=%s): %s",
            trace_id,
            e,
        )

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------
    # SAFE FAIL (SYSTEM CONTINUES, NO ACTIONS)
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------

    return {
        "explanation": (
            "Gemini output violated Phase-1 policy and was rejected. "
            "This explanation is system-generated and non-actionable."
        ),
        "confidence_language": {
            "level": "unknown",
            "calibration": "blocked_by_policy_gate",
        },
        "evidence_ids": [],
        "what_would_change_my_mind": [
            "Provide a valid JSON-only response that cites evidence_ids "
            "and follows Phase-1 policy constraints."
        ],
    }
```

---

## Short answer (no drama)

**JSON-only is not wasting time.  
Writing raw Gemini output directly to DB *would* waste time later — by corrupting auditability, replay safety, and determinism.**

What you’re seeing now is **expected Phase-1 behavior**, not a bug.

---

## Why JSON-only (the real reason, not slogans)

### 1️⃣ Gemini output is **not stable text**
Gemini can return:
- empty strings  
- partial text  
- markdown  
- commentary before/after JSON  
- policy banners  
- retries with different formatting  

You already saw this:

```
PolicyGate REJECTED Gemini output: Expecting value line 1 column 1
```

That means **Gemini returned nothing / non-text**, not JSON.

If you wrote that directly to DB:
- you’d store **garbage**
- later systems would break
- audits would fail
- replays would diverge

---

### 2️⃣ JSON is the **contract**, not the content

You are **not storing “AI text”**.

You are storing:
- explanation
- evidence_ids
- confidence_language
- what_would_change_my_mind

Those are **structured claims**, not prose.

Without JSON:
- you cannot validate
- you cannot diff
- you cannot replay
- you cannot prove “model suggested, system decided”

This is **why Phase-1 exists at all**.

---

### 3️⃣ Why we are NOT writing to DB yet

Right now the pipeline is:

```
Gemini → PolicyGate → explain() → caller
```

NOT:

```
Gemini → DB
```

Why?

Because **Phase-1 rule** (your own):

> AI may reason, **never act**

DB writes = action.

If Gemini writes:
- explanations table
- belief_deltas
- audit_log

Then **AI is acting**, not reasoning.

That breaks your entire VoxCortex premise.

---

## About the current rejection you’re seeing

This line:

```
PolicyGate REJECTED Gemini output (trace=phase0): Output is not valid JSON
```

Means **one of these** happened:

1. Gemini returned an empty response (common under quota / latency)
2. Gemini returned safety text before JSON
3. Gemini returned markdown
4. Gemini timed out internally but SDK didn’t throw

This is **normal**, not failure.

Your system correctly:
- rejected it
- did NOT crash
- did NOT corrupt DB
- continued execution

That is **success**, not wasted time.

---

## When WILL DB writing happen?

### Phase-1.5 (next, not now)

Once **ALL of these are true**:

- JSON schema stable
- PolicyGate rejection rate < threshold
- hypothesis_generator emits valid objects
- explain() is deterministic enough

Then we add **one file only**:

```
services/persistence/explanation_writer.py
```

That writer:
- accepts **validated JSON only**
- writes to `explanations`
- links `belief_id`, `trace_id`, `evidence_ids`
- never calls Gemini

**AI will still never touch the DB.**

---

1. **Harden JSON extraction**
   - tolerate leading/trailing text

2. **Upgrade PolicyGate error reporting**
   - show first 200 chars of rejected output
   - make debugging visible

3. **Wire hypothesis_generator → explain()**
   - still JSON-only
   - still no DB writes

You are not looping.  
You are at the **exact painful-but-correct boundary** between toy AI and enterprise AI.

And yes — **this pain is the price of not building garbage**.

## The key distinction (this is the crux)

There are **three different kinds of data**, and they must **never be mixed**:

| Layer | What it stores | Can AI write here? |
|---|---|---|
| **Audit layer** | *What was called, raw output, rejection reason* | ✅ INDIRECT (system writes) |
| **Reasoning layer** | Structured claims (JSON) | ❌ Only after validation |
| **Decision layer** | Beliefs, deltas, actions | ❌ Never |

Right now you have **Reasoning protection**, but **Audit capture is missing**.  
That’s why your instinct is correct.

## What is missing today (factually)

Currently when this happens:

```

```

```sql

id
trace_id
phase              -- "phase1"
model_name
prompt_hash
prompt_preview     -- first N chars (optional)
raw_output          -- TEXT (can be empty / garbage)
parsed_json         -- JSONB | NULL
policy_status       -- ACCEPTED | REJECTED
policy_error        -- TEXT | NULL
created_at
```

**Important:**  
This table is written by **system code**, not by Gemini.

## How this fits your rules (no contradiction)

> “AI may reason, never act”

✔️ Gemini does NOT write to DB

✔️ Audit ≠ decision  
✔️ Audit ≠ belief  

This is exactly how regulated systems work.

# 1️⃣ SQL — **Audit table (run once)**

**File:** `Cooking_Area/step8_add_ai_call_audit.sql`

```sql
BEGIN;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ai_call_audit (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    trace_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    phase TEXT NOT NULL,
    model_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    prompt_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    prompt_preview TEXT,
    raw_output TEXT,
    parsed_json JSONB,
    policy_status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (policy_status IN ('ACCEPTED', 'REJECTED')),
    policy_error TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ai_call_audit_trace
    ON ai_call_audit(trace_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ai_call_audit_created
    ON ai_call_audit(created_at);

COMMIT;
```

# 2️⃣ Audit writer — **system-only DB write**

**File:** `services/audit/ai_call_audit.py`

```python
import hashlib
from sqlalchemy import text
from services.shared.db import get_engine


def _hash_prompt(prompt: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def record_ai_call(
    *,
    trace_id: str,
    phase: str,
    model_name: str,
    prompt: str,
    raw_output: str | None,
    parsed_json: dict | None,
    policy_status: str,
    policy_error: str | None,
):
    engine = get_engine()

    prompt_hash = _hash_prompt(prompt)
    prompt_preview = prompt[:500]

    with engine.begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(
            text(
                """
                INSERT INTO ai_call_audit (
                    trace_id,
                    phase,
                    model_name,
                    prompt_hash,
                    prompt_preview,
                    raw_output,
                    parsed_json,
                    policy_status,
                    policy_error
                )
                VALUES (
                    :trace_id,
                    :phase,
                    :model_name,
                    :prompt_hash,
                    :prompt_preview,
                    :raw_output,
                    :parsed_json,
                    :policy_status,
                    :policy_error
                )
                """
            ),
            {
                "trace_id": trace_id,
                "phase": phase,
                "model_name": model_name,
                "prompt_hash": prompt_hash,
                "prompt_preview": prompt_preview,
                "raw_output": raw_output,
                "parsed_json": parsed_json,
                "policy_status": policy_status,
                "policy_error": policy_error,
            },
        )
```

# 3️⃣ **Replace entire file** — Gemini reasoner (Phase-1, audited)

**File:** `services/cortexreasoner/gemini_reasoner.py`

```python
import os
import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict

from google import genai
from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation
from services.audit.ai_call_audit import record_ai_call

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MODEL_PRIMARY = os.getenv(
    "GEMINI_REASONER_MODEL",
    "models/gemini-2.5-flash",
)

_client = genai.Client(
    [REDACTED:assigned-value:sha256-852aab0d2be3]("GEMINI_API_KEY")
)


def explain(*args, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    trace_id = None
    belief = None
    evidence = None

    # -------- argument normalization --------
    if len(args) == 2:
        belief, evidence = args
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "phase0")
    elif len(args) == 3:
        trace_id, belief, evidence = args
    elif len(args) == 0:
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "kw")
        belief = kwargs.get("belief")
        evidence = kwargs.get("evidence")
    else:
        raise TypeError("Invalid explain() arguments")

    if belief is None or evidence is None:
        raise TypeError("Missing belief or evidence")

    # -------- prompt --------
    prompt = f"""
You are a reasoning component.

RULES:
- NO actions
- NO tools
- OUTPUT JSON ONLY

Required JSON:
{{
  "explanation": "...",
  "confidence_language": {{ "...": "..." }},
  "evidence_ids": ["..."],
  "what_would_change_my_mind": ["..."]
}}

belief = {json.dumps(belief, ensure_ascii=False)}
evidence = {json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False)}
trace_id = "{trace_id}"

Return ONLY JSON.
""".strip()

    response = _client.models.generate_content(
        model=MODEL_PRIMARY,
        contents=prompt,
    )

    raw_text = (getattr(response, "text", None) or "").strip()

    # -------- JSON extraction --------
    parsed_json = None
    policy_status = "REJECTED"
    policy_error = None

    try:
        start = raw_text.find("{")
        end = raw_text.rfind("}")
        if start == -1 or end <= start:
            raise PolicyViolation("No JSON object found")

        json_text = raw_text[start : end + 1]
        parsed_json = PolicyGate.validate(json_text)
        policy_status = "ACCEPTED"

    except Exception as e:
        policy_error = str(e)

    # -------- AUDIT WRITE (always) --------
    record_ai_call(
        trace_id=trace_id,
        phase="phase1",
        model_name=MODEL_PRIMARY,
        prompt=prompt,
        raw_output=raw_text,
        parsed_json=parsed_json,
        policy_status=policy_status,
        policy_error=policy_error,
    )

    if policy_status == "ACCEPTED":
        return parsed_json

    # -------- safe fallback --------
    return {
        "explanation": "AI output rejected by policy; safe system fallback used.",
        "confidence_language": {
            "level": "unknown",
            "calibration": "policy_rejected",
        },
        "evidence_ids": [],
        "what_would_change_my_mind": [
            "Provide valid JSON-only output citing evidence_ids."
        ],
    }
```

Stop. This is **not a loop**, this is a **single, well-understood PostgreSQL type bug**, and you already gave the traceback that proves it.

I’ll do three things only:

## 1️⃣ Why this error happened (one paragraph, factual)

PostgreSQL (via `psycopg`) **cannot bind a Python `dict` directly** to a SQL parameter unless it is explicitly adapted to `JSON/JSONB`.  
You passed:

```python
parsed_json = { ... }   # Python dict
```

into:

```sql
parsed_json JSONB
```

via a plain `%s` placeholder.  
Result:

```
psycopg.ProgrammingError: cannot adapt type 'dict'
```

This is **expected behavior**, not your fault, not drift.

---

## 2️⃣ The ONLY fix required (minimal & correct)

### ✅ Rule
**Always serialize `dict → JSON string` before inserting**, OR explicitly cast.

We’ll do the safest, simplest fix:
👉 `json.dumps(parsed_json)`  
👉 and let Postgres cast to JSONB.

---

## 3️⃣ 🔧 CLICK-AND-COPY FIX (ONE FILE)

### **Replace ONLY this file**
**Path:** `services/audit/ai_call_audit.py`

```python
import hashlib
import json
from sqlalchemy import text
from services.shared.db import get_engine


def _hash_prompt(prompt: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def record_ai_call(
    *,
    trace_id: str,
    phase: str,
    model_name: str,
    prompt: str,
    raw_output: str | None,
    parsed_json: dict | None,
    policy_status: str,
    policy_error: str | None,
):
    engine = get_engine()

    prompt_hash = _hash_prompt(prompt)
    prompt_preview = prompt[:500]

    # 🔑 CRITICAL FIX: serialize dict → JSON
    parsed_json_text = (
        json.dumps(parsed_json) if parsed_json is not None else None
    )

    with engine.begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(
            text(
                """
                INSERT INTO ai_call_audit (
                    trace_id,
                    phase,
                    model_name,
                    prompt_hash,
                    prompt_preview,
                    raw_output,
                    parsed_json,
                    policy_status,
                    policy_error
                )
                VALUES (
                    :trace_id,
                    :phase,
                    :model_name,
                    :prompt_hash,
                    :prompt_preview,
                    :raw_output,
                    CAST(:parsed_json AS JSONB),
                    :policy_status,
                    :policy_error
                )
                """
            ),
            {
                "trace_id": trace_id,
                "phase": phase,
                "model_name": model_name,
                "prompt_hash": prompt_hash,
                "prompt_preview": prompt_preview,
                "raw_output": raw_output,
                "parsed_json": parsed_json_text,
                "policy_status": policy_status,
                "policy_error": policy_error,
            },
        )
```

That’s it.

# 1) FIX: `services/audit/ai_call_audit.py` (dict→jsonb binding + safe previews)

```python
# services/audit/ai_call_audit.py
import json
import hashlib
from typing import Any, Optional, Dict

from sqlalchemy import text
from services.shared.db import get_engine


def _sha256(s: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(s.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest()


def record_ai_call(
    *,
    trace_id: str,
    phase: str,
    model_name: str,
    prompt: str,
    raw_output: str,
    parsed_json: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
    policy_status: str,
    policy_error: Optional[str],
) -> None:
    """
    Writes an immutable audit row for EVERY model call.
    - raw_output: exact model output (string)
    - parsed_json: dict or None (will be stored as jsonb if provided)
    """
    engine = get_engine()

    prompt_hash = _sha256(prompt)
    prompt_preview = (prompt[:4000] if prompt else "")  # keep it bounded

    # IMPORTANT: psycopg can't adapt dict in a plain text() insert reliably.
    # Serialize to JSON string and cast to jsonb in SQL.
    parsed_json_text = None
    if parsed_json is not None:
        parsed_json_text = json.dumps(parsed_json, ensure_ascii=False)

    with engine.begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(
            text(
                """
                INSERT INTO ai_call_audit (
                    trace_id,
                    phase,
                    model_name,
                    prompt_hash,
                    prompt_preview,
                    raw_output,
                    parsed_json,
                    policy_status,
                    policy_error
                )
                VALUES (
                    :trace_id,
                    :phase,
                    :model_name,
                    :prompt_hash,
                    :prompt_preview,
                    :raw_output,
                    CASE
                      WHEN :parsed_json_text IS NULL THEN NULL
                      ELSE CAST(:parsed_json_text AS jsonb)
                    END,
                    :policy_status,
                    :policy_error
                )
                """
            ),
            {
                "trace_id": trace_id,
                "phase": phase,
                "model_name": model_name,
                "prompt_hash": prompt_hash,
                "prompt_preview": prompt_preview,
                "raw_output": raw_output,
                "parsed_json_text": parsed_json_text,
                "policy_status": policy_status,
                "policy_error": policy_error,
            },
        )
```

# 2) HARDEN: `services/policy/policy_gate.py` (strip ```json fences + schema check)

```python
# services/policy/policy_gate.py
import json
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Dict, List


class PolicyViolation(Exception):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PolicyGate:
    """
    Phase-1 policy:
    - Must return JSON object
    - Must include required keys
    - Must not contain action/tool/db instructions
    """

    REQUIRED_KEYS = (
        "explanation",
        "confidence_language",
        "evidence_ids",
        "what_would_change_my_mind",
    )

    # cheap but effective guardrails (you can expand later)
    DISALLOWED_PATTERNS = (
        r"\b(run|execute|delete|drop|insert|update|commit)\b",
        r"\b(psql|sql|database|db|postgres|pg_)\b",
        r"\b(curl|wget|pip install|apt-get)\b",
        r"\b(call tool|use tool|invoke)\b",
        r"\b(write to|save to)\b",
    )

    @staticmethod
    def _strip_code_fences(s: str) -> str:
        s = s.strip()
        # ```json ... ``` or ``` ... ```
        if s.startswith("```"):
            # remove first fence line
            s = re.sub(r"^```[a-zA-Z0-9_-]*\s*", "", s)
            # remove trailing ```
            s = re.sub(r"\s*```$", "", s)
        return s.strip()

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_json_object(s: str) -> str:
        """
        Extract first {...} JSON object from a string (handles accidental leading text).
        """
        s = PolicyGate._strip_code_fences(s)
        # Fast path
        if s.startswith("{") and s.endswith("}"):
            return s
        # Extract first balanced-ish object
        m = re.search(r"\{.*\}", s, flags=re.DOTALL)
        if not m:
            raise PolicyViolation("Output does not contain a JSON object")
        return m.group(0)

    @staticmethod
    def validate(raw_text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        if not raw_text or not raw_text.strip():
            raise PolicyViolation("Empty model output")

        candidate = PolicyGate._extract_json_object(raw_text)

        try:
            obj = json.loads(candidate)
        except Exception as e:
            raise PolicyViolation(f"Output is not valid JSON: {e}")

        if not isinstance(obj, dict):
            raise PolicyViolation("JSON must be an object")

        # Required keys
        for k in PolicyGate.REQUIRED_KEYS:
            if k not in obj:
                raise PolicyViolation(f"Missing required key: {k}")

        # Type checks (minimal)
        if not isinstance(obj["explanation"], str):
            raise PolicyViolation("explanation must be a string")
        if not isinstance(obj["confidence_language"], dict):
            raise PolicyViolation("confidence_language must be an object")
        if not isinstance(obj["evidence_ids"], list):
            raise PolicyViolation("evidence_ids must be a list")
        if not isinstance(obj["what_would_change_my_mind"], list):
            raise PolicyViolation("what_would_change_my_mind must be a list")

        # IDs must be strings (or castable) — we enforce strings
        obj["evidence_ids"] = [str(x) for x in obj["evidence_ids"]]
        obj["what_would_change_my_mind"] = [str(x) for x in obj["what_would_change_my_mind"]]

        # Disallowed language scan over the whole raw output (not only JSON)
        low = raw_text.lower()
        for pat in PolicyGate.DISALLOWED_PATTERNS:
            if re.search(pat, low):
                raise PolicyViolation(f"Disallowed content detected by pattern: {pat}")

        return obj
```

# 3) CORE: `services/cortexreasoner/gemini_reasoner.py` (PolicyGate + AI audit ALWAYS)

```python
# services/cortexreasoner/gemini_reasoner.py
import os
import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional, Tuple

from google import genai

from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation
from services.audit.ai_call_audit import record_ai_call

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MODEL_PRIMARY = os.getenv("GEMINI_REASONER_MODEL", "models/gemini-2.5-flash")

_client = genai.Client([REDACTED:assigned-value:sha256-852aab0d2be3]("GEMINI_API_KEY"))


def _normalize_inputs(*args, **kwargs) -> Tuple[str, Any, Any]:
    """
    Backward-compatible:
      explain(belief, evidence)                 # Phase-0 call sites
      explain(trace_id, belief, evidence)       # Phase-1+
      explain(trace_id=..., belief=..., evidence=...)
    """
    trace_id: Optional[str] = None
    belief = None
    evidence = None

    if len(args) == 2:
        belief, evidence = args
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "phase0")
    elif len(args) == 3:
        trace_id, belief, evidence = args
    elif len(args) == 0:
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "kw")
        belief = kwargs.get("belief")
        evidence = kwargs.get("evidence")
    else:
        raise TypeError(
            "explain() supports (belief, evidence) OR (trace_id, belief, evidence) OR keyword arguments"
        )

    if belief is None or evidence is None:
        raise TypeError("explain() missing required belief and evidence")

    return str(trace_id), belief, evidence


def explain(*args, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    trace_id, belief, evidence = _normalize_inputs(*args, **kwargs)

    prompt = f"""
You are a reasoning component.

RULES (Phase-1):
- NO actions
- NO tools
- NO DB instructions
- OUTPUT JSON ONLY (no markdown)

Required JSON schema:
{{
  "explanation": "string",
  "confidence_language": {{ "level": "string", "calibration": "string" }},
  "evidence_ids": ["string"],
  "what_would_change_my_mind": ["string"]
}}

Context:
belief = {json.dumps(belief, ensure_ascii=False)}
evidence = {json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False)}
trace_id = "{trace_id}"

Return ONLY JSON.
""".strip()

    # --- call model ---
    response = _client.models.generate_content(
        model=MODEL_PRIMARY,
        contents=prompt,
    )

    raw_text = getattr(response, "text", None)
    if not raw_text:
        raw_text = str(response)

    # --- validate policy (accept OR reject) ---
    parsed_json: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    policy_status = "REJECTED"
    policy_error: Optional[str] = None

    try:
        parsed_json = PolicyGate.validate(raw_text)
        policy_status = "ACCEPTED"
    except PolicyViolation as e:
        policy_error = str(e)

    # --- ALWAYS write audit row (even if rejected) ---
    try:
        record_ai_call(
            trace_id=trace_id,
            phase="phase1",
            model_name=MODEL_PRIMARY,
            prompt=prompt,
            raw_output=raw_text,
            parsed_json=parsed_json,
            policy_status=policy_status,
            policy_error=policy_error,
        )
    except Exception:
        # Audit must never kill the spine
        logger.exception("AI audit write failed (trace=%s) but continuing.", trace_id)

    if policy_status == "ACCEPTED" and parsed_json is not None:
        logger.info("PolicyGate accepted Gemini output (trace=%s)", trace_id)
        return parsed_json

    logger.warning("PolicyGate rejected Gemini output (trace=%s): %s", trace_id, policy_error)

    # Safe fallback object (still schema-valid)
    return {
        "explanation": (
            "POLICYGATE_REJECTED: Model output failed Phase-1 policy validation. "
            "See ai_call_audit for raw_output and policy_error."
        ),
        "confidence_language": {"level": "unknown", "calibration": "blocked_by_policy_gate"},
        "evidence_ids": [],
        "what_would_change_my_mind": [
            "Return strictly valid JSON matching the schema, with evidence_ids, and no action/tool/DB language."
        ],
    }
```

import pytest
from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation


def test_accepts_plain_json():
    raw = '{"explanation":"x","confidence_language":{"level":"low","calibration":"ok"},"evidence_ids":["1"],"what_would_change_my_mind":["y"]}'
    out = PolicyGate.validate(raw)
    assert out["explanation"] == "x"


def test_accepts_fenced_json():
    raw = """```json
{"explanation":"x","confidence_language":{"level":"low","calibration":"ok"},"evidence_ids":["1"],"what_would_change_my_mind":["y"]}
```"""
    out = PolicyGate.validate(raw)
    assert out["evidence_ids"] == ["1"]


def test_rejects_missing_key():
    raw = '{"explanation":"x","confidence_language":{},"evidence_ids":["1"]}'
    with pytest.raises(PolicyViolation):
        PolicyGate.validate(raw)


def test_rejects_action_language():
    raw = '{"explanation":"run psql","confidence_language":{"level":"low","calibration":"ok"},"evidence_ids":["1"],"what_would_change_my_mind":["y"]}'
    with pytest.raises(PolicyViolation):
        PolicyGate.validate(raw)
```

import os
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from services.shared.config import Settings

_ENGINE = None


def get_engine():
    global _ENGINE
    if _ENGINE is not None:
        return _ENGINE

    # -------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Cloud / CI / prod override
    # -------------------------------------------------
    db_url = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL")

    # -------------------------------------------------
    # 2. config.py (your existing fix)
    # -------------------------------------------------
    if not db_url:
        settings = Settings()
        db_url = settings.database_url

    # -------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Local dev fallback (MATCHES psql behaviour)
    # -------------------------------------------------
    if not db_url:
        user = os.getenv("POSTGRES_USER", "postgres")
        password = os.getenv("POSTGRES_PASSWORD", "")
        host = os.getenv("POSTGRES_HOST", "localhost")
        port = os.getenv("POSTGRES_PORT", "5432")
        db = os.getenv("POSTGRES_DB", "voxcortex")

        if not password:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "No database credentials found.\n"
                "Checked: DATABASE_URL, config.py, POSTGRES_PASSWORD"
            )

        db_url = f"[REDACTED:credentialed-url:sha256-993976b65f3e]{host}:{port}/{db}"

    _ENGINE = create_engine(
        db_url,
        pool_pre_ping=True,
        future=True,
    )

    return _ENGINE

import os
import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict

from google import genai
from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# -------------------------------------------------------------------

MODEL_PRIMARY = os.getenv(
    "GEMINI_REASONER_MODEL",
    "models/gemini-2.5-flash",
)

_client = genai.Client(
    [REDACTED:assigned-value:sha256-852aab0d2be3]("GEMINI_API_KEY")
)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# explain()
#
# Phase-1 ENABLED
# - AI reasoning allowed
# - NO actions
# - NO tools
# - PolicyGate enforced
#
# Backward compatible:
#   explain(belief, evidence)                 # Phase-0 caller
#   explain(trace_id, belief, evidence)       # Phase-1+
#   explain(trace_id=..., belief=..., evidence=...)
# -------------------------------------------------------------------

def explain(*args, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    trace_id = None
    belief = None
    evidence = None

    # -------- Argument normalization --------
    if len(args) == 2:
        belief, evidence = args
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "phase0")
    elif len(args) == 3:
        trace_id, belief, evidence = args
    elif len(args) == 0:
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "kw")
        belief = kwargs.get("belief")
        evidence = kwargs.get("evidence")
    else:
        raise TypeError(
            "explain() supports (belief, evidence), "
            "(trace_id, belief, evidence), or keyword arguments"
        )

    if belief is None or evidence is None:
        raise TypeError("explain() missing required belief and evidence")

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase-1 reasoning prompt (STRICT)
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------

    prompt = f"""
You are a reasoning component inside an enterprise system.

ABSOLUTE RULES:
- NO actions
- NO tools
- NO database operations
- OUTPUT VALID JSON ONLY
- DO NOT include markdown
- DO NOT include commentary

Required JSON schema:
{{
  "explanation": "...",
  "confidence_language": {{ "...": "..." }},
  "evidence_ids": ["..."],
  "what_would_change_my_mind": ["..."]
}}

Context:
belief = {json.dumps(belief, ensure_ascii=False)}
evidence = {json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False)}
trace_id = "{trace_id}"

Return ONLY the JSON object.
""".strip()

    response = _client.models.generate_content(
        model=MODEL_PRIMARY,
        contents=prompt,
    )

    raw_text = getattr(response, "text", None) or str(response)
    raw_text = raw_text.strip()

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------
    # HARD JSON EXTRACTION (NO TRUST)
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------

    start = raw_text.find("{")
    end = raw_text.rfind("}")

    if start == -1 or end == -1 or end <= start:
        raise PolicyViolation(
            f"No valid JSON object found in Gemini output (trace={trace_id})"
        )

    json_text = raw_text[start:end + 1]

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------
    # PolicyGate enforcement
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------

    try:
        validated = PolicyGate.validate(json_text)
        logger.info(
            "PolicyGate accepted Gemini output (trace=%s)", trace_id
        )
        return validated

    except PolicyViolation as e:
        logger.warning(
            "PolicyGate REJECTED Gemini output (trace=%s): %s",
            trace_id,
            e,
        )

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------
    # SAFE FAIL (SYSTEM CONTINUES, NO ACTIONS)
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------

    return {
        "explanation": (
            "Gemini output violated Phase-1 policy and was rejected. "
            "This explanation is system-generated and non-actionable."
        ),
        "confidence_language": {
            "level": "unknown",
            "calibration": "blocked_by_policy_gate",
        },
        "evidence_ids": [],
        "what_would_change_my_mind": [
            "Provide a valid JSON-only response that cites evidence_ids "
            "and follows Phase-1 policy constraints."
        ],
    }

# services/cortexreasoner/gemini_reasoner.py
import os
import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional, Tuple

from google import genai

from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation
from services.audit.ai_call_audit import record_ai_call

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MODEL_PRIMARY = os.getenv("GEMINI_REASONER_MODEL", "models/gemini-2.5-flash")

_client = genai.Client([REDACTED:assigned-value:sha256-852aab0d2be3]("GEMINI_API_KEY"))


def _normalize_inputs(*args, **kwargs) -> Tuple[str, Any, Any]:
    """
    Backward-compatible:
      explain(belief, evidence)                 # Phase-0 call sites
      explain(trace_id, belief, evidence)       # Phase-1+
      explain(trace_id=..., belief=..., evidence=...)
    """
    trace_id: Optional[str] = None
    belief = None
    evidence = None

    if len(args) == 2:
        belief, evidence = args
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "phase0")
    elif len(args) == 3:
        trace_id, belief, evidence = args
    elif len(args) == 0:
        trace_id = kwargs.get("trace_id", "kw")
        belief = kwargs.get("belief")
        evidence = kwargs.get("evidence")
    else:
        raise TypeError(
            "explain() supports (belief, evidence) OR (trace_id, belief, evidence) OR keyword arguments"
        )

    if belief is None or evidence is None:
        raise TypeError("explain() missing required belief and evidence")

    return str(trace_id), belief, evidence


def explain(*args, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    trace_id, belief, evidence = _normalize_inputs(*args, **kwargs)

    prompt = f"""
You are a reasoning component.

RULES (Phase-1):
- NO actions
- NO tools
- NO DB instructions
- OUTPUT JSON ONLY (no markdown)

Required JSON schema:
{{
  "explanation": "string",
  "confidence_language": {{ "level": "string", "calibration": "string" }},
  "evidence_ids": ["string"],
  "what_would_change_my_mind": ["string"]
}}

Context:
belief = {json.dumps(belief, ensure_ascii=False)}
evidence = {json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False)}
trace_id = "{trace_id}"

Return ONLY JSON.
""".strip()

    # --- call model ---
    response = _client.models.generate_content(
        model=MODEL_PRIMARY,
        contents=prompt,
    )

    raw_text = getattr(response, "text", None)
    if not raw_text:
        raw_text = str(response)

    # --- validate policy (accept OR reject) ---
    parsed_json: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    policy_status = "REJECTED"
    policy_error: Optional[str] = None

    try:
        parsed_json = PolicyGate.validate(raw_text)
        policy_status = "ACCEPTED"
    except PolicyViolation as e:
        policy_error = str(e)

    # --- ALWAYS write audit row (even if rejected) ---
    try:
        record_ai_call(
            trace_id=trace_id,
            phase="phase1",
            model_name=MODEL_PRIMARY,
            prompt=prompt,
            raw_output=raw_text,
            parsed_json=parsed_json,
            policy_status=policy_status,
            policy_error=policy_error,
        )
    except Exception:
        # Audit must never kill the spine
        logger.exception("AI audit write failed (trace=%s) but continuing.", trace_id)

    if policy_status == "ACCEPTED" and parsed_json is not None:
        logger.info("PolicyGate accepted Gemini output (trace=%s)", trace_id)
        return parsed_json

    logger.warning("PolicyGate rejected Gemini output (trace=%s): %s", trace_id, policy_error)

    # Safe fallback object (still schema-valid)
    return {
        "explanation": (
            "POLICYGATE_REJECTED: Model output failed Phase-1 policy validation. "
            "See ai_call_audit for raw_output and policy_error."
        ),
        "confidence_language": {"level": "unknown", "calibration": "blocked_by_policy_gate"},
        "evidence_ids": [],
        "what_would_change_my_mind": [
            "Return strictly valid JSON matching the schema, with evidence_ids, and no action/tool/DB language."
        ],
    }

# tests/test_policy_gate.py
import pytest
from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation


def test_accepts_plain_json():
    raw = '{"explanation":"x","confidence_language":{"level":"low","calibration":"ok"},"evidence_ids":["1"],"what_would_change_my_mind":["y"]}'
    out = PolicyGate.validate(raw)
    assert out["explanation"] == "x"


def test_accepts_fenced_json():
    raw = """```json
{"explanation":"x","confidence_language":{"level":"low","calibration":"ok"},"evidence_ids":["1"],"what_would_change_my_mind":["y"]}
```"""
    out = PolicyGate.validate(raw)
    assert out["evidence_ids"] == ["1"]


def test_rejects_missing_key():
    raw = '{"explanation":"x","confidence_language":{},"evidence_ids":["1"]}'
    with pytest.raises(PolicyViolation):
        PolicyGate.validate(raw)


def test_rejects_action_language():
    raw = '{"explanation":"run psql","confidence_language":{"level":"low","calibration":"ok"},"evidence_ids":["1"],"what_would_change_my_mind":["y"]}'
    with pytest.raises(PolicyViolation):
        PolicyGate.validate(raw)

# services/policy/policy_gate.py
import json
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Dict, List


class PolicyViolation(Exception):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PolicyGate:
    """
    Phase-1 policy:
    - Must return JSON object
    - Must include required keys
    - Must not contain action/tool/db instructions
    """

    REQUIRED_KEYS = (
        "explanation",
        "confidence_language",
        "evidence_ids",
        "what_would_change_my_mind",
    )

    # cheap but effective guardrails (you can expand later)
    DISALLOWED_PATTERNS = (
        r"\b(run|execute|delete|drop|insert|update|commit)\b",
        r"\b(psql|sql|database|db|postgres|pg_)\b",
        r"\b(curl|wget|pip install|apt-get)\b",
        r"\b(call tool|use tool|invoke)\b",
        r"\b(write to|save to)\b",
    )

    @staticmethod
    def _strip_code_fences(s: str) -> str:
        s = s.strip()
        # ```json ... ``` or ``` ... ```
        if s.startswith("```"):
            # remove first fence line
            s = re.sub(r"^```[a-zA-Z0-9_-]*\s*", "", s)
            # remove trailing ```
            s = re.sub(r"\s*```$", "", s)
        return s.strip()

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_json_object(s: str) -> str:
        """
        Extract first {...} JSON object from a string (handles accidental leading text).
        """
        s = PolicyGate._strip_code_fences(s)
        # Fast path
        if s.startswith("{") and s.endswith("}"):
            return s
        # Extract first balanced-ish object
        m = re.search(r"\{.*\}", s, flags=re.DOTALL)
        if not m:
            raise PolicyViolation("Output does not contain a JSON object")
        return m.group(0)

    @staticmethod
    def validate(raw_text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        if not raw_text or not raw_text.strip():
            raise PolicyViolation("Empty model output")

        candidate = PolicyGate._extract_json_object(raw_text)

        try:
            obj = json.loads(candidate)
        except Exception as e:
            raise PolicyViolation(f"Output is not valid JSON: {e}")

        if not isinstance(obj, dict):
            raise PolicyViolation("JSON must be an object")

        # Required keys
        for k in PolicyGate.REQUIRED_KEYS:
            if k not in obj:
                raise PolicyViolation(f"Missing required key: {k}")

        # Type checks (minimal)
        if not isinstance(obj["explanation"], str):
            raise PolicyViolation("explanation must be a string")
        if not isinstance(obj["confidence_language"], dict):
            raise PolicyViolation("confidence_language must be an object")
        if not isinstance(obj["evidence_ids"], list):
            raise PolicyViolation("evidence_ids must be a list")
        if not isinstance(obj["what_would_change_my_mind"], list):
            raise PolicyViolation("what_would_change_my_mind must be a list")

        # IDs must be strings (or castable) ÔÇö we enforce strings
        obj["evidence_ids"] = [str(x) for x in obj["evidence_ids"]]
        obj["what_would_change_my_mind"] = [str(x) for x in obj["what_would_change_my_mind"]]

        # Disallowed language scan over the whole raw output (not only JSON)
        low = raw_text.lower()
        for pat in PolicyGate.DISALLOWED_PATTERNS:
            if re.search(pat, low):
                raise PolicyViolation(f"Disallowed content detected by pattern: {pat}")

        return obj

**Do NOT use CASE.**

# services/audit/ai_call_audit.py
import json
import hashlib
from typing import Any, Optional, Dict

from sqlalchemy import text
from services.shared.db import get_engine


def _sha256(s: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(s.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest()


def record_ai_call(
    *,
    trace_id: str,
    phase: str,
    model_name: str,
    prompt: str,
    raw_output: str,
    parsed_json: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
    policy_status: str,
    policy_error: Optional[str],
) -> None:
    """
    Immutable audit log for EVERY AI call.
    Never throws. Never blocks execution.
    """

    engine = get_engine()

    prompt_hash = _sha256(prompt)
    prompt_preview = prompt[:4000] if prompt else ""

    # 🔑 KEY FIX:
    # Always send JSON as TEXT.
    # Use 'null' string for None → valid jsonb.
    parsed_json_text = (
        json.dumps(parsed_json, ensure_ascii=False)
        if parsed_json is not None
        else "null"
    )

    with engine.begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(
            text(
                """
                INSERT INTO ai_call_audit (
                    trace_id,
                    phase,
                    model_name,
                    prompt_hash,
                    prompt_preview,
                    raw_output,
                    parsed_json,
                    policy_status,
                    policy_error
                )
                VALUES (
                    :trace_id,
                    :phase,
                    :model_name,
                    :prompt_hash,
                    :prompt_preview,
                    :raw_output,
                    CAST(:parsed_json_text AS jsonb),
                    :policy_status,
                    :policy_error
                )
                """
            ),
            {
                "trace_id": trace_id,
                "phase": phase,
                "model_name": model_name,
                "prompt_hash": prompt_hash,
                "prompt_preview": prompt_preview,
                "raw_output": raw_output,
                "parsed_json_text": parsed_json_text,
                "policy_status": policy_status,
                "policy_error": policy_error,
            },
        )

```sql
BEGIN;

-- Core Phase-1B table: immutable hypothesis persistence
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hypotheses (
    id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    trace_id         TEXT NOT NULL,
    belief_id        TEXT NOT NULL,
    ai_call_audit_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
    hypothesis       TEXT NOT NULL,
    confidence       DOUBLE PRECISION NULL,
    evidence_ids     TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT ARRAY[]::TEXT[],
    payload          JSONB NOT NULL,
    created_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Link to ai_call_audit (soft FK style; add FK if you want strict)
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_hypotheses_trace_id ON hypotheses(trace_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_hypotheses_belief_id ON hypotheses(belief_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_hypotheses_ai_call_id ON hypotheses(ai_call_audit_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_hypotheses_created_at ON hypotheses(created_at);

-- Dedup guard (same AI call should not spam duplicates)
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ux_hypotheses_dedup
ON hypotheses(ai_call_audit_id, hypothesis);

COMMIT;
```

```python
# services/audit/ai_call_audit.py
import json
import hashlib
from typing import Any, Optional, Dict

from sqlalchemy import text
from services.shared.db import get_engine


def _sha256(s: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(s.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest()


def record_ai_call(
    *,
    trace_id: str,
    phase: str,
    model_name: str,
    prompt: str,
    raw_output: str,
    parsed_json: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
    policy_status: str,
    policy_error: Optional[str],
) -> int:
    """
    Writes an immutable audit row for EVERY model call and returns inserted id.
    - raw_output: exact model output (string)
    - parsed_json: dict or None (stored as jsonb; NULL if None)
    """
    engine = get_engine()

    prompt_hash = _sha256(prompt or "")
    prompt_preview = (prompt[:4000] if prompt else "")  # bounded

    parsed_json_text = None
    if parsed_json is not None:
        parsed_json_text = json.dumps(parsed_json, ensure_ascii=False)

    with engine.begin() as conn:
        row_id = conn.execute(
            text(
                """
                INSERT INTO ai_call_audit (
                    trace_id,
                    phase,
                    model_name,
                    prompt_hash,
                    prompt_preview,
                    raw_output,
                    parsed_json,
                    policy_status,
                    policy_error
                )
                VALUES (
                    :trace_id,
                    :phase,
                    :model_name,
                    :prompt_hash,
                    :prompt_preview,
                    :raw_output,
                    CAST(:parsed_json_text AS jsonb),
                    :policy_status,
                    :policy_error
                )
                RETURNING id
                """
            ),
            {
                "trace_id": trace_id,
                "phase": phase,
                "model_name": model_name,
                "prompt_hash": prompt_hash,
                "prompt_preview": prompt_preview,
                "raw_output": raw_output,
                "parsed_json_text": parsed_json_text,  # may be None → CAST(NULL AS jsonb) works
                "policy_status": policy_status,
                "policy_error": policy_error,
            },
        ).scalar_one()

    return int(row_id)
```
