# 2) Replace PolicyGate (multi-schema, backward compatible)

✅ **REPLACE**: `services/policy/policy_gate.py`

```python
# services/policy/policy_gate.py
import json
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple


class PolicyViolation(Exception):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PolicyGate:
    """
    Phase-1 PolicyGate (enterprise, non-negotiable):
    - Output must be JSON object (we can extract from fenced or noisy text)
    - Schema must match a declared schema family (explain/options/regulatory/translate)
    - Must NOT contain action/tool/DB instructions anywhere in raw output
    """

    # Disallowed language scan over whole raw output (not only JSON)
    DISALLOWED_PATTERNS = (
        r"\b(run|execute|delete|drop|insert|update|commit)\b",
        r"\b(psql|sql|database|db|postgres|pg_)\b",
        r"\b(curl|wget|pip install|apt-get)\b",
        r"\b(call tool|use tool|invoke)\b",
        r"\b(write to|save to)\b",
    )

    # Schema families (strict required keys)
    SCHEMAS: Dict[str, Tuple[str, ...]] = {
        # Backward compatible default: what gemini_reasoner currently returns
        "explain": (
            "explanation",
            "confidence_language",
            "evidence_ids",
            "what_would_change_my_mind",
        ),
        # Option generator: includes "options"
        "options": (
            "explanation",
            "confidence_language",
            "evidence_ids",
            "what_would_change_my_mind",
            "options",
        ),
        # Regulatory drafts: includes "drafts"
        "regulatory": (
            "explanation",
            "confidence_language",
            "evidence_ids",
            "what_would_change_my_mind",
            "drafts",
        ),
        # Translation/normalization: includes "canonical_event"
        "translate": (
            "explanation",
            "confidence_language",
            "evidence_ids",
            "what_would_change_my_mind",
            "canonical_event",
        ),
    }

    @staticmethod
    def _strip_code_fences(s: str) -> str:
        s = (s or "").strip()
        if s.startswith("```"):
            s = re.sub(r"^```[a-zA-Z0-9_-]*\s*", "", s)
            s = re.sub(r"\s*```$", "", s)
        return s.strip()

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_json_object(s: str) -> str:
        s = PolicyGate._strip_code_fences(s)
        if s.startswith("{") and s.endswith("}"):
            return s
        m = re.search(r"\{.*\}", s, flags=re.DOTALL)
        if not m:
            raise PolicyViolation("Output does not contain a JSON object")
        return m.group(0)

    @staticmethod
    def _scan_disallowed(raw_text: str) -> None:
        low = (raw_text or "").lower()
        for pat in PolicyGate.DISALLOWED_PATTERNS:
            if re.search(pat, low):
                raise PolicyViolation(f"Disallowed content: {pat}")

    @staticmethod
    def validate(raw_text: str, schema: str = "explain") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Backward-compatible:
          PolicyGate.validate(raw_text) -> schema="explain"
        Extended:
          PolicyGate.validate(raw_text, schema="options"/"regulatory"/"translate")
        """
        if not raw_text or not str(raw_text).strip():
            raise PolicyViolation("Empty model output")

        PolicyGate._scan_disallowed(raw_text)

        candidate = PolicyGate._extract_json_object(raw_text)
        try:
            obj = json.loads(candidate)
        except Exception as e:
            raise PolicyViolation(f"Output is not valid JSON: {e}")

        if not isinstance(obj, dict):
            raise PolicyViolation("JSON must be an object")

        if schema not in PolicyGate.SCHEMAS:
            raise PolicyViolation(f"Unknown schema: {schema}")

        required = PolicyGate.SCHEMAS[schema]
        for k in required:
            if k not in obj:
                raise PolicyViolation(f"Missing required key: {k}")

        # minimal type checks shared across schemas
        if not isinstance(obj["explanation"], str):
            raise PolicyViolation("explanation must be a string")
        if not isinstance(obj["confidence_language"], dict):
            raise PolicyViolation("confidence_language must be an object")
        if not isinstance(obj["evidence_ids"], list):
            raise PolicyViolation("evidence_ids must be a list")
        if not isinstance(obj["what_would_change_my_mind"], list):
            raise PolicyViolation("what_would_change_my_mind must be a list")

        # normalize list elements to strings
        obj["evidence_ids"] = [str(x) for x in obj["evidence_ids"]]
        obj["what_would_change_my_mind"] = [str(x) for x in obj["what_would_change_my_mind"]]

        # schema-specific checks
        if schema == "options":
            if not isinstance(obj["options"], list):
                raise PolicyViolation("options must be a list")
        if schema == "regulatory":
            if not isinstance(obj["drafts"], list):
                raise PolicyViolation("drafts must be a list")
        if schema == "translate":
            if not isinstance(obj["canonical_event"], dict):
                raise PolicyViolation("canonical_event must be an object")

        return obj
```

import os

_client = genai.Client([REDACTED:assigned-value:sha256-852aab0d2be3]("GEMINI_API_KEY"))


def generate_options(
    *,
    trace_id: str,
    belief_id: str,
    belief: Dict[str, Any],
    evidence: Dict[str, Any],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Phase-1 (NO ACTIONS):
      Produce mitigation/decision OPTIONS only.
      NEVER execute. NEVER instruct tools. JSON-only validated.
    """
    prompt = f"""
You are an enterprise reasoning component.

ABSOLUTE RULES (Phase-1):
- NO actions
- NO tools
- NO database instructions
- NO operational commands
- OUTPUT VALID JSON ONLY (no markdown, no prose outside JSON)

Required JSON schema:
{{
  "explanation": "string",
  "confidence_language": {{ "level": "string", "calibration": "string" }},
  "evidence_ids": ["string"],
  "what_would_change_my_mind": ["string"],
  "options": [
    {{
      "option_id": "OPT-1",
      "title": "string",
      "summary": "string",
      "pros": ["string"],
      "cons": ["string"],
      "risks": ["string"],
      "reversibility": "low|medium|high",
      "effort": "low|medium|high",
      "time_horizon": "immediate|short|medium|long",
      "dependencies": ["string"]
    }}
  ]
}}

Context:
trace_id = "{trace_id}"
belief_id = "{belief_id}"
belief = {json.dumps(belief, ensure_ascii=False)}
evidence = {json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False)}

Return ONLY the JSON object.
""".strip()

    response = _client.models.generate_content(model=MODEL_PRIMARY, contents=prompt)
    raw_text = (getattr(response, "text", None) or str(response)).strip()

    parsed_json: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    policy_status = "REJECTED"
    policy_error: Optional[str] = None

    try:
        parsed_json = PolicyGate.validate(raw_text, schema="options")
        policy_status = "ACCEPTED"
    except PolicyViolation as e:
        policy_error = str(e)

    # audit always
    try:
        record_ai_call(
            trace_id=trace_id,
            phase="phase1",
            model_name=MODEL_PRIMARY,
            prompt=prompt,
            raw_output=raw_text,
            parsed_json=parsed_json,
            policy_status=policy_status,
            policy_error=policy_error,
        )
    except Exception:
        logger.exception("AI audit write failed (trace=%s) but continuing.", trace_id)

    if policy_status == "ACCEPTED" and parsed_json is not None:
        return parsed_json

    # safe fallback (schema-valid)
    return {
        "explanation": (
            "POLICYGATE_REJECTED: option generation blocked. "
            "See ai_call_audit for raw_output and policy_error."
        ),
        "confidence_language": {"level": "unknown", "calibration": "blocked_by_policy_gate"},
        "evidence_ids": [],
        "what_would_change_my_mind": [
            "Return strictly valid JSON matching the options schema with no action/tool/DB language."
        ],
        "options": [],
    }

# 4) Conflict Resolver (P1-13, deterministic)

✅ **NEW FILE**: `services/conflictresolver/resolver.py`

```python
# services/conflictresolver/resolver.py
import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from sqlalchemy import text
from services.shared.db import get_engine


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ConflictSet:
    belief_id: str
    trace_id: str
    winner_hypothesis_id: Optional[int]
    ranked: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    notes: str


def resolve_conflicts_for_belief(
    *,
    trace_id: str,
    belief_id: str,
    top_k: int = 5,
) -> ConflictSet:
    """
    Deterministic conflict resolver:
      - Reads hypotheses for (trace_id, belief_id)
      - Ranks by (confidence desc, recency desc, evidence overlap desc)
      - Returns a conflict-set object (NO DB writes required)
    """

    engine = get_engine()
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            text(
                """
                SELECT hypothesis_id, hypothesis_text, confidence, evidence_ids, created_at
                FROM hypotheses
                WHERE trace_id = :trace_id AND belief_id = :belief_id
                ORDER BY created_at DESC
                LIMIT 200
                """
            ),
            {"trace_id": trace_id, "belief_id": belief_id},
        ).fetchall()

    if not rows:
        return ConflictSet(
            belief_id=belief_id,
            trace_id=trace_id,
            winner_hypothesis_id=None,
            ranked=[],
            notes="No hypotheses found for belief_id/trace_id.",
        )

    # Normalize
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for r in rows:
        evidence_ids = r[3] or []
        items.append(
            {
                "hypothesis_id": int(r[0]),
                "hypothesis_text": str(r[1]),
                "confidence": float(r[2]) if r[2] is not None else 0.0,
                "evidence_ids": [str(x) for x in evidence_ids],
                "created_at": r[4],
            }
        )

    # Evidence overlap heuristic: hypotheses that share more evidence with others are "more supported"
    # Compute overlap score = sum of intersections with other hypotheses
    ev_sets = [set(x["evidence_ids"]) for x in items]
    overlap_scores = []
    for i, s in enumerate(ev_sets):
        score = 0
        for j, t in enumerate(ev_sets):
            if i == j:
                continue
            score += len(s.intersection(t))
        overlap_scores.append(score)

    for i, it in enumerate(items):
        it["overlap_score"] = overlap_scores[i]

    ranked = sorted(
        items,
        key=lambda x: (x["confidence"], x["created_at"], x["overlap_score"]),
        reverse=True,
    )[: max(1, top_k)]

    winner = ranked[0]["hypothesis_id"] if ranked else None

    return ConflictSet(
        belief_id=belief_id,
        trace_id=trace_id,
        winner_hypothesis_id=winner,
        ranked=ranked,
        notes="Ranked deterministically by confidence, recency, evidence-overlap.",
    )
```

✅ **NEW FILE**: `services/conflictresolver/__init__.py` (if not already created)

```python

```

```python
# services/cortexreasoner/phase1_orchestrator.py
import json
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

from sqlalchemy import text

from services.shared.db import get_engine
from services.cortexreasoner.hypothesis_store import persist_hypothesis_from_ai
from services.cortexreasoner.option_generator import generate_options
from services.cortexreasoner.regulatory_draft_generator import draft_regulatory_notice
from services.cortexreasoner.translator_normalizer import translate_and_normalize_event
from services.conflictresolver.resolver import resolve_conflicts_for_belief


def run_phase1_decisions(*, trace_id: str, belief_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Phase-1 Orchestrator:
      - NO ACTIONS EVER
      - Executes Phase-1 cognition modules
      - Deterministic conflict resolution (no AI)
    """
    engine = get_engine()

    # Load current belief row (dict)
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        belief_row = conn.execute(
            text(
                """
                SELECT belief_id, trace_id, subject, hypothesis, confidence, evidence_ids, updated_at
                FROM beliefs
                WHERE belief_id = :belief_id
                """
            ),
            {"belief_id": belief_id},
        ).mappings().first()

    if not belief_row:
        return {"ok": False, "error": "belief not found"}

    belief = dict(belief_row)

    # Evidence packet (minimal) - in your worker you already have sha/signature
    evidence = {
        "evidence_ids": belief.get("evidence_ids", []) or [],
        "subject": belief.get("subject", ""),
    }

    # P1-12 options
    options = generate_options(
        trace_id=trace_id,
        belief_id=str(belief_id),
        belief=belief,
        evidence=evidence,
    )

    # P1-14 regulatory drafts (example jurisdictions, extend later)
    regulatory = draft_regulatory_notice(
        trace_id=trace_id,
        belief_id=str(belief_id),
        jurisdiction="UK",
        belief=belief,
        evidence=evidence,
    )

    # P1-15 translation/normalization sample (no source text here by default)
    translation = translate_and_normalize_event(
        trace_id=trace_id,
        belief_id=str(belief_id),
        source_text=f"Service incident summary: {belief.get('hypothesis','')}",
        source_lang="en",
        target_lang="en",
        hints={"subject": belief.get("subject", "")},
    )

    # P1-13 conflict resolution (deterministic)
    conflict_set = resolve_conflicts_for_belief(
        trace_id=trace_id,
        belief_id=str(belief_id),
        top_k=5,
    )

    # Write a single audit_log entry for Phase-1 cognition completion (deterministic write)
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(
            text(
                """
                INSERT INTO audit_log (trace_id, actor, action, details, created_at)
                VALUES (:trace_id, :actor, :action, CAST(:details AS jsonb), now())
                """
            ),
            {
                "trace_id": trace_id,
                "actor": "phase1_orchestrator",
                "action": "phase1_cognition_complete",
                "details": json.dumps(
                    {
                        "belief_id": str(belief_id),
                        "options_count": len(options.get("options", [])),
                        "regulatory_count": len(regulatory.get("drafts", [])),
                        "translation_conf": translation.get("canonical_event", {}).get("translation_confidence", 0.0),
                        "conflict_winner": conflict_set.winner_hypothesis_id,
                        "conflict_ranked": conflict_set.ranked,
                    },
                    ensure_ascii=False,
                ),
            },
        )

    return {
        "ok": True,
        "belief_id": str(belief_id),
        "options": options,
        "regulatory": regulatory,
        "translation": translation,
        "conflicts": {
            "winner_hypothesis_id": conflict_set.winner_hypothesis_id,
            "ranked": conflict_set.ranked,
            "notes": conflict_set.notes,
        },
    }
```

# 8) Fix your HealthCheck “No module named services” forever

✅ **REPLACE**: `HealthCheck/06_phase1_decision_check.py`

```python
# HealthCheck/06_phase1_decision_check.py
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from services.cortexreasoner.phase1_orchestrator import run_phase1_decisions
from services.shared.db import get_engine
from sqlalchemy import text


def main():
    print("=== PHASE-1 DECISION CHECK ===")

    # Get latest belief_id
    engine = get_engine()
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        row = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT belief_id, trace_id FROM beliefs ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 1")
        ).fetchone()

    if not row:
        print("No beliefs found. Run worker first.")
        return

    belief_id, trace_id = row[0], row[1]
    out = run_phase1_decisions(trace_id=trace_id, belief_id=str(belief_id))
    print("Decision OK:", out.get("ok"))
    print("belief_id:", out.get("belief_id"))
    print("options_count:", len(out.get("options", {}).get("options", [])))
    print("regulatory_count:", len(out.get("regulatory", {}).get("drafts", [])))
    print("translation_conf:", out.get("translation", {}).get("canonical_event", {}).get("translation_confidence"))
    print("conflict_winner:", out.get("conflicts", {}).get("winner_hypothesis_id"))

    print("✅ PHASE-1 DECISION CHECK COMPLETE")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

# 9) EvalLab (tests) — no toys

### 9.1 Update existing PolicyGate tests

✅ **REPLACE**: `tests/test_policy_gate.py`

```python
# tests/test_policy_gate.py
import pytest
from services.policy.policy_gate import PolicyGate, PolicyViolation


def test_accepts_plain_json_explain_default():
    raw = '{"explanation":"x","confidence_language":{"level":"low","calibration":"ok"},"evidence_ids":["1"],"what_would_change_my_mind":["y"]}'
    out = PolicyGate.validate(raw)
    assert out["explanation"] == "x"


def test_accepts_fenced_json_explain():
    raw = """```json
{"explanation":"x","confidence_language":{"level":"low","calibration":"ok"},"evidence_ids":["1"],"what_would_change_my_mind":["y"]}
```"""
    out = PolicyGate.validate(raw)
    assert out["evidence_ids"] == ["1"]


def test_accepts_options_schema():
    raw = """{
      "explanation":"x",
      "confidence_language":{"level":"low","calibration":"ok"},
      "evidence_ids":["1"],
      "what_would_change_my_mind":["y"],
      "options":[{"option_id":"OPT-1","title":"t","summary":"s","pros":[],"cons":[],"risks":[],"reversibility":"high","effort":"low","time_horizon":"short","dependencies":[]}]
    }"""
    out = PolicyGate.validate(raw, schema="options")
    assert isinstance(out["options"], list)


def test_rejects_action_language_anywhere():
    raw = '{"explanation":"run psql","confidence_language":{"level":"low","calibration":"ok"},"evidence_ids":["1"],"what_would_change_my_mind":["y"]}'
    with pytest.raises(PolicyViolation):
        PolicyGate.validate(raw)
```

# tests/evals/test_conflictresolver.py
from services.conflictresolver.resolver import resolve_conflicts_for_belief


def test_conflictresolver_handles_no_rows(monkeypatch):
    # We don't hit DB here; just ensure function exists and returns structure.
    # If DB has no hypotheses for given IDs, it should return empty ranked list.
    out = resolve_conflicts_for_belief(trace_id="__no__", belief_id="__no__", top_k=3)
    assert out.ranked == []
    assert out.winner_hypothesis_id is None
